# Clock variants

[catalog index](../CATALOG.md)

A variant is a clock with the same period as a smaller clock (its host: fewer blocks, or as many and better on a variation) that it beats on no variation (the same on-time, pulses, start and robustness, no lower box, no shorter warmup, slime-free only if the host is), whatever its block kinds (palette alone is no distinction); the galleries show only the hosts. 1762 variants of 440 hosts, by period. Generated by `mctas clock catalog`.
